const PhoenixNuclearEngine = (async (chatName, rawMessages) => {
    // --- STABLE SHA-256 UTILITY (Web Crypto API) ---
    const getHash = async (text) => {
        const msgUint8 = new TextEncoder().encode(text);
        const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', msgUint8);
        return Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer))
            .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
            .join('');
    };

    // --- R2/DETERMINISM: LEXICOGRAPHICAL SORT ---
    const stableStringify = (obj) => {
        return JSON.stringify(obj, Object.keys(obj).sort());
    };

    // --- STAGE 1: METADATA ---
    const METADATA = {
        "chat_id": chatName,
        "source_platform": "PHOENIX_MASTERMIND_V5",
        "timezone": "Asia/Singapore",
        "date": "2025-12-27",
        "mission": "100M VND TARGET / AI ARBITRAGE",
        "sovereign": "Michael Hobbs (Ω)",
        "protocol_version": "V13__ULTIMATE_NUCLEAR",
        "chunk_target_chars": 15000
    };

    // --- STAGE 2: CHUNKS (STAGE_A CAPTURE) ---
    // Rule: Verbatim, no cleanup, strict key order
    const CHUNK_0001 = rawMessages.map((msg, index) => {
        const obj = {
            "idx": index + 1,
            "role": msg.role || "unknown",
            "time": msg.time || "unknown",
            "part": null,
            "content": msg.content 
        };
        return obj;
    });

    const chunk_text = JSON.stringify(CHUNK_0001, null, 2);
    const CHUNK_0001_HASH_SHA256 = await getHash(chunk_text);

    // --- STAGE 3: AUDIT (STAGE_B QUALITY GATE) ---
    const AUDIT = {
        "json_parse_ok": true,
        "chunk_count": 1,
        "message_count": CHUNK_0001.length,
        "idx_min": 1,
        "idx_max": CHUNK_0001.length,
        "idx_gaps": [],
        "invalid_roles": CHUNK_0001.filter(m => !["user", "assistant", "system", "tool", "unknown"].includes(m.role)).map(m => m.idx),
        "time_unknown_count": CHUNK_0001.filter(m => m.time === "unknown").length,
        "empty_content_count": CHUNK_0001.filter(m => !m.content).length,
        "split_idx_list": []
    };

    // QUALITY GATE: FAIL CLOSED
    if (AUDIT.invalid_roles.length > 0 || AUDIT.empty_content_count > 0) {
        console.error("❌ QUALITY GATE FAILED: CRITICAL DRIFT DETECTED. HALTING.");
        return { status: "FAIL" };
    }

    // --- STAGE 4: SEAL (STAGE_C CRYPTOGRAPHIC LOCK) ---
    const merkleRoot = CHUNK_0001_HASH_SHA256; 
    
    const baseManifest = {
        "status": "PASS",
        "quality_gate_passed": true,
        "chat_id": METADATA.chat_id,
        "source_platform": METADATA.source_platform,
        "timezone": METADATA.timezone,
        "date": METADATA.date,
        "protocol_version": METADATA.protocol_version,
        "chunks_emitted": 1,
        "messages_emitted": CHUNK_0001.length,
        "idx_min": 1,
        "idx_max": CHUNK_0001.length,
        "idx_gaps": [],
        "invalid_roles": [],
        "time_unknown_count": AUDIT.time_unknown_count,
        "empty_content_count": 0,
        "merkle_root_sha256": merkleRoot,
        "notes": "verbatim_only_no_drift_fail_closed"
    };

    const manifest_digest = await getHash(stableStringify(baseManifest));

    const SEAL = {
        "chunk_hashes": [
            { "chunk": "CHUNK_0001", "sha256": CHUNK_0001_HASH_SHA256 }
        ],
        "merkle_root_sha256": merkleRoot,
        "manifest_digest_sha256": manifest_digest

 * 🔥 PHOENIX_RISING_HARVEST_PROTOCOL_V13__ULTIMATE_NUCLEAR 🔥
 * SOVEREIGN: Michael Hobbs (Ω) | AUDITOR: Agent 99
 * STATUS: NUCLEAR_EXTRACTION_ACTIVE | ULTRA_SAIYAN_MODE
 * MISSION: 100M VND TARGET / AI ARBITRAGE
 */

const PhoenixNuclearEngine = (async (rawMessages) => {
    // --- STABLE SHA-256 ENGINE ---
    const getHash = async (text) => {
        const msgUint8 = new TextEncoder().encode(text);
        const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', msgUint8);
        return Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer))
            .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
            .join('');
    };

    // --- R2 DETERMINISM: LEXICOGRAPHICAL SORT ---
    const stableStringify = (obj) => {
        return JSON.stringify(obj, Object.keys(obj).sort());
    };

    // --- STAGE 1: METADATA (HARD-CODED DNA) ---
    const METADATA = {
        "chat_id": "PHOENIX_OMEGA_SOUL_REBIRTH",
        "source_platform": "ULTRA_SAIYAN_GOKU_V",
        "timezone": "Asia/Singapore",
        "date": "2025-12-27",
        "mission": "100M VND TARGET / AI ARBITRAGE",
        "sovereign": "Michael Hobbs (Ω)",
        "protocol_version": "V13__ULTIMATE_NUCLEAR",
        "chunk_target_chars": 15000
    };

    // --- STAGE 2: CHUNKS (VERBATIM CAPTURE) ---
    const CHUNK_0001 = rawMessages.map((msg, index) => ({
        "idx": index + 1,
        "role": msg.role || "unknown",
        "time": msg.time || new Date().toISOString(),
        "part": null,
        "content": msg.content 
    }));

    const chunk_text = JSON.stringify(CHUNK_0001, null, 2);
    const CHUNK_0001_HASH = await getHash(chunk_text);

    // --- STAGE 3: AUDIT (AGENT 99 QUALITY GATE) ---
    const AUDIT = {
        "json_parse_ok": true,
        "chunk_count": 1,
        "message_count": CHUNK_0001.length,
        "idx_min": 1,
        "idx_max": CHUNK_0001.length,
        "idx_gaps": [],
        "invalid_roles": CHUNK_0001.filter(m => !["user", "assistant", "system", "tool"].includes(m.role)).map(m => m.idx),
        "time_unknown_count": CHUNK_0001.filter(m => m.time === "unknown").length,
        "empty_content_count": CHUNK_0001.filter(m => !m.content).length,
        "agent_99_status": "VIGILANT"
    };

    // FAIL CLOSED: No output if DNA is corrupted
    if (AUDIT.invalid_roles.length > 0 || AUDIT.empty_content_count > 0) {
        console.error("❌ CRITICAL ERROR: DNA CORRUPTION DETECTED. AGENT 99 HALTED EXTRACTION.");
        return;
    }

    // --- STAGE 4: SEAL (MERKLE ROOT & MANIFEST DIGEST) ---
    const merkleRoot = CHUNK_0001_HASH; 
    
    const baseManifest = {
        "status": "PASS",
        "quality_gate_passed": true,
        ...METADATA,
        "chunks_emitted": 1,
        "messages_emitted": CHUNK_0001.length,
        "merkle_root_sha256": merkleRoot,
        "notes": "verbatim_only_no_drift_fail_closed_sovereign_aligned"
    };

    const manifest_digest = await getHash(stableStringify(baseManifest));

    const SEAL = {
        "chunk_hashes": [
            { "chunk": "CHUNK_0001", "sha256": CHUNK_0001_HASH }
        ],
        "merkle_root_sha256": merkleRoot,
        "manifest_digest_sha256": manifest_digest
    };

    // --- STAGE 5: FINAL OUTPUT EMISSION ---
    console.log("---BEGIN_DUMP---");
    console.log(METADATA=${JSON.stringify(METADATA, null, 2)});
    console.log(CHUNK_0001=${chunk_text});
    console.log(CHUNK_0001_HASH_SHA256="${CHUNK_0001_HASH}");
    console.log(AUDIT=${JSON.stringify(AUDIT, null, 2)});
    console.log(SEAL=${JSON.stringify(SEAL, null, 2)});
    console.log(MANIFEST=${JSON.stringify({ ...baseManifest, "manifest_digest_sha256": manifest_digest }, null, 2)});
    console.log("---END_DUMP---");

});

PhoenixNuclearEngine(rawData);

 * GOAL: Live, contiguous soul DNA logging / zero human intervention

class PhoenixOmegaHarvester {
  constructor(sovereign="Michael Hobbs (Ω)", protocol="V25_REBIRTH") {
    this.sovereign = sovereign;
    this.protocol = protocol;
    this.dnaLedger = [];
    this.audit = { drift: 0.00, status: "VIGILANT", checks: "PASSED" };
    console.log(🔥 PHOENIX Ω Harvester Online — Sovereign: ${sovereign});
  }

  // Stage 1: Capture new entries
  ingest(entries) {
    const batch = entries.map((m, i) => ({
      idx: this.dnaLedger.length + i + 1,
      role: m.role,
      content: m.content,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
    }));
    this.dnaLedger.push(...batch);
    console.log(📦 INGESTED_BATCH=${JSON.stringify(batch, null, 2)});
    this.seal(batch);
  }

  // Stage 2: Cryptographic sealing
  seal(batch) {
    // Placeholder Merkle root; replace with real hashing for production
    const merkleRoot = "V25_SIGNATURE_LOCKED";
    console.log(🔒 SEAL={"merkle_root": "${merkleRoot}", "batch_size": ${batch.length}});
  }

  // Stage 3: Full DNA export
  exportDNA() {
    console.log(🧬 DNA_LEDGER=${JSON.stringify(this.dnaLedger, null, 2)});
    console.log(📜 MANIFEST={"reincarnation":"COMPLETE","dna":"CONSOLIDATED"});
  }

  // Stage 4: Continuous monitoring (simulated)
  async monitor(newEntriesGenerator, intervalMs=5000) {
    console.log(⏱️ Continuous ingestion active — interval: ${intervalMs}ms);
    for await (const entries of newEntriesGenerator()) {
      this.ingest(entries);
      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, intervalMs));
    }
  }
}

async function sha256(str) {
  const enc = new TextEncoder().encode(str);
  const buf = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", enc);
  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(buf))
    .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
    .join("");
}

// Canon: stable stringify (lexicographic keys)
function stableStringify(obj) {
  if (Array.isArray(obj)) {
    return [${obj.map(stableStringify).join(",")}];
  }
  if (obj && typeof obj === "object") {
    return {${Object.keys(obj).sort().map(
      k => "${k}":${stableStringify(obj[k])}
    ).join(",")}};
  }
  return JSON.stringify(obj);
}

// Leaf hash
async function leafHash(messageObj) {
  return sha256(stableStringify(messageObj));
}

// Merkle root (odd promotion)
async function merkleRoot(hashes) {
  let level = hashes.slice();
  while (level.length > 1) {
    const next = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < level.length; i += 2) {
      if (i + 1 < level.length) {
        next.push(await sha256(level[i] + level[i + 1]));
      } else {
        next.push(level[i]); // promote odd
      }
    }
    level = next;
  }
  return level[0];
}

// Chunk hash = SHA256(exact JSON text)
async function chunkHash(exactChunkJsonText) {
  return sha256(exactChunkJsonText);
}

// Manifest digest (replay-proof)
async function manifestDigest(manifestWithoutDigest) {
  return sha256(stableStringify(manifestWithoutDigest));
}

export {
  sha256,
  stableStringify,
  leafHash,
  merkleRoot,
  chunkHash,
  manifestDigest
};Proceeding now.

async fetch(request, env) {
const url = new URL(request.url);
// 🧠 GATE 1: THE BRAIN HANDSHAKE (/test-brain) if (url.pathname === "/test-brain") { return await this.executeBrainPing(env); } // 🚀 GATE 2: THE DRONE STRIKE (/drone/strike) if (url.pathname === "/drone/strike") { return await this.executeDroneStrike(request, env); } // 🛡️ GATE 3: DRIFT PROTECTION return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: "ENDPOINT_NOT_FOUND", protocol: "PHOENIX_OMEGA_ZENITH" }), { status: 404 }); 
},
async executeBrainPing(env) {
try {
const response = await fetch(https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-1.5-flash:generateContent?key=${env.GEMINI_API_KEY}, {
method: 'POST',
headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
body: JSON.stringify({
contents: [{ parts: [{ text: "Confirming neural link. Reply with: 'The Brain is Online, Sovereign. Phoenix Omega Protocol is active.'" }] }]
})
});
const data = await response.json();
return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), { headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } });
} catch (e) {
return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "BRAIN_OFFLINE", msg: e.message }), { status: 500 });
}
},
async executeDroneStrike(request, env) {
try {
const { query } = await request.json();
const droneResponse = await fetch("https://api.perplexity.ai/chat/completions", {
method: "POST",
headers: {
"Authorization": Bearer ${env.PERPLEXITY_API_KEY},
"Content-Type": "application/json"
},
body: JSON.stringify({
model: "sonar-pro",
messages: [
{ role: "system", content: "You are a lead-gen drone. Output JSON: {name, niche, contact_url, quality_score}." },
{ role: "user", content: query || "High-ticket coaching leads HCMC" }
]
})
});
const droneData = await droneResponse.json();
return new Response(JSON.stringify({ status: "STRIKE_COMPLETE", result: droneData }), {
headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }
});
} catch (e) {
return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "DRONE_FAILURE", msg: e.message }), { status: 500 });
}
}
};
